|\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`）\\n- **ML 栈**: transformers/trl/vllm/accelerate 全部预装\\n- **Solvers**: Prover9 (LADR 2009-11A 源码编译), SWI-Prolog 8.4.2 (`/usr/bin/swipl`), Z3 4.16.0.0\\n- **模型**: Qwen3-4B 已下载（7.6GB，通过 ModelScope）\\n- **代码路径**: `/root/solver-trace-ablation/`\\n- **磁盘**: 系统盘 30GB (22G free)，数据盘 `/root/autodl-tmp/` 250GB (142G free)\\n\\n### 决策记录\\n- Qwen3-4B 用 ModelScope 下载（HuggingFace 直连可能慢）\\n- Prover9 从 LADR 源码编译（apt 无包）\\n- 94GB VRAM 单卡跑 Qwen3-4B 完全无压力\\n\\n## 数据 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### 数据源变更：ProofWriter → FOLIO\\n- ProofWriter 原始数据无 FOL 列，改用 `tasksource/folio`（1204 条人工标注 FOL）\\n- Phase 0 子集 100 条，训练子集 500 条\\n- 34% 数据含 XOR (⊕) 符号，已在 `_normalize_fol()` 中修复展开\\n\\n### 数据约束 (2026-05-14, D009)\\n- **C001**: 所有 plan001/plan013 实验统一使用 `train_500_seed42.jsonl`，不再用 `proofwriter_fol_500.jsonl`（消除数据差异 confound）\\n\\n## Phase 0 关键发现 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### Base Model ISR Baseline (sta-phase0-baseline)\\n- **Prove rate**: 12% overall, 18.2% excluding XOR-affected samples\\n- **Mean ISR**: 0.064 (very low — base model formalizations mostly unprovable)\\n- **ISR 区分度**: proved 样本 ISR 0.125–0.889\\n\\n### Ground-Truth ISR Baseline (sta-gt-isr-baseline)\\n- **GT prove rate**: 18% (limited by Prover9 only proving entailment; true=50%, false=4%, unknown=0%)\\n- **GT mean ISR**: 0.5588 (median 0.6125, range 0.14–1.00)\\n- **Model vs GT gap**: ISR 0.064 vs 0.559 = 8.7x 差距，STA 有强区分力\\n\\n### Prover9 局限性\\n- Prover9 只能证明 entailment（conclusion 从 premises 推出）\\n- `false` 和 `unknown` 类问题几乎无法证明（false 4%, unknown 0%）\\n\\n## Phase 1 关键经验 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### fast_isr_fol 失败教训 (r4)\\n- `fast_isr_fol()` 是二值 tautology gate (0/1)，Qwen3-4B 不产生 tautology → ISR ≡ prove_rate → 两条件零差异\\n- **教训：binary ISR 无法提供有意义的 reward shaping 信号，必须用连续 ISR**\\n\\n### D010 Reward 公式转向 (2026-05-14)\\n- **旧公式（废弃）**: r = α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1 → ISR<1 时永远是 penalty\\n- **新公式**: r = r_exec · (1 + β·ISR), β>0 → ISR 只能 bonus，不能 penalty\\n\\n## Held-out eval_sta 全量结果 (2026-05-14) — 关键\\n\\n### 九组 eval 对比（含 plan016 1000步 + plan018 random β=0.1）\\n\\n| 实验 | Reward | Steps | eval PR | eval ISR | proved_only_ISR | n_components | 退化模式 |\\n|------|--------|-------|---------|----------|-----------------|--------------|----------|\\n| plan001-s42 | exec-only | 500 | **93%** | 0.123 | 0.132 | 6.58 | ✅ Genuine proofs |\\n| plan001-s43 | exec-only | 500 | **14%** | 0.000 | 0.000 | N/A | ❌ 长输出退化 |\\n| plan001-s44 | exec-only | 500 | **98%** | 0.570 | 0.582 | 2.04 | ❌ Tautological collapse |\\n| **plan016** | **exec-only** | **1000** | **96%** | **0.003** | **0.003** | **18.55** | **❌ 矛盾爆炸崩塌** |\\n| **plan002-b01** | **additive ISR β=0.1** | 500 | **81%** | **0.337** | **0.415** | **5.58** | **✅ Genuine + improved** |\\n| **plan018** | **additive random β=0.1** | 500 | **79%** | **0.104** | **0.131** | **8.46** | **⚠️ 冗余但无坍缩** |\\n| plan002-b03 | additive ISR β=0.3 | 500 | 18% | 0.026 | 0.143 | 10.67 | ❌ 过拟合+低泛化 |\\n| plan017 | additive random β=0.3 | 500 | 48% | 0.045 | 0.094 | 9.6 | ❌ 复杂但无用 |\\n| plan013 | convex random | 500 | 100% | 0.535 | 0.535 | 2.04 | ❌ Tautological hacking |\\n\\n### β=0.1 ISR vs Random 同剂量对照 (plan002-b01 vs plan018, D031 定论) — 重大发现\\n\\n**ISR β=0.1 提供 genuine structural guidance，非 variance regularization。**\\n\\n- PR 几乎相同 (0.81 vs 0.79) → β=0.1 噪声不影响证明能力\\n- ISR 差 3.2x (0.337 vs 0.104) → ISR 信号引导结构改善\\n- Components 5.58 vs 8.46 → ISR 组更精简\\n- random 组 36.7% proved 样本 ISR=0（无结构证明）\\n\\n### ISR-as-Reward β 剂量响应完整图谱\\n\\n| β | ISR reward PR | Random reward PR | ISR reward ISR | Random reward ISR | 结论 |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| 0.1 | 0.81 | 0.79 | **0.337** | 0.104 | ISR >> random (3.2x) |\\n| 0.3 | 0.18 | 0.48 | 0.026 | 0.045 | ISR << random (Goodhart) |\\n\\n**剂量依赖**：低 β ISR 有结构价值；高 β ISR 触发 Goodhart 效应反而比 random 更差。\\n\\n### plan016 延长训练发现 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n**ISR 崩塌随训练持续恶化，无回升信号。**\\n- 500步 ISR=0.123 → 1000步 ISR=0.003 (-97.8%)\\n- Windowed ISR 单调递减：step 200 peak 0.1115 → step 1000 低至 0.0144\\n- 退化模式：非 2-component 同义反复，而是矛盾爆炸（大量无关前提嵌入矛盾）\\n- Components 6.58→18.55 (+182%)\\n- 论文意义：exec-only 延长训练是 reward hacking 恶化的干净消融证据\\n\\n### 跨 seed 退化分类 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n| Seed | 退化模式 | 特征 | 诊断信号 |\\n|------|----------|------|----------|\\n| s42 | ✅ Genuine | PR=93%, components=6.58 | 唯一健康 seed |\\n| s43 | ❌ Length explosion | PR=14%, 87% hit max_new_tokens | D029: 不重训 |\\n| s44 | ❌ Tautological collapse | PR=98%, components=2.04 | ISR=0.5 artifact |\\n\\n### 关键教训\\n1. **跨 seed 极不稳定**: exec-only reward landscape 方差极大\\n2. **ISR + component count 联合诊断**能区分 genuine proof vs tautological hack\\n3. **ISR-as-reward 有 sweet spot**: β=0.1 有效，β≥0.3 有害（Goodhart's Law）\\n4. **训练 PR vs eval PR 巨大鸿沟**: 训练 PR 不反映 held-out 泛化能力\\n5. **ISR β=0.1 vs random β=0.1**: ISR 3.2x 差异证明 ISR 信号有结构价值（非 variance regularization）\\n\\n## Downstream NLI 验证失败 (2026-05-14, plan014)\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, correct) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\n- 根因：模型严重 true-bias (false accuracy=0%), NLI accuracy 天花板 34.1%\\n- D022/D029: 降级为 Limitation/Future Work\\n\\n## Goodhart 文献定位 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n### 核心 novelty（Goodhart 文献调研确认）\\n1. **首个在 neurosymbolic verification reward 上刻画 Goodhart 效应**：已有工作研究 RLHF preference RM (Gao et al. 2023)、game 环境 (Pan et al. 2022)、code execution (CodeRL) 的 reward hacking，无人研究 logical solver output 作为 RL reward 时的 Goodhart\\n2. **Verification-based auxiliary reward 的剂量响应曲线**：首个 β sweep 展示 solver-based metric (ISR) 的倒 U 型行为。已有 dose-response 研究在 RLHF preference (Rafailov et al. 2024) 或 robotics (Wang et al. 2025)，不在 formal verification\\n3. **Dose-dependent qualitative shift**：β=0.1→0.3 从 PR 81%→18% 的质变（非 smooth degradation），连接 Pan et al. 的 dose-dependent dynamics 但在 neurosymbolic 上下文。注意：仅 2 个 β 值，不足以称 \\\"phase transition\\\"，需更细粒度 β sweep\\n4. ~~Graded vs binary verification reward 的 Goodhart 易感性差异~~：**已降级为 discussion/future work**——无 binary ISR ablation 实验支撑，不能作为 novelty claim\\n\\n### Goodhart 分类\\n- 我们的 high-β ISR collapse = **extremal Goodhart** (Manheim & Garrabrant 2019)：policy 移到 ISR↑ 但 PR↓ 的 OOD 区域\\n- 可能有 **causal Goodhart** 成分：ISR 和 quality 的相关性在优化压力下断裂\\n\\n### 预期 reviewer 问题\\n1. \\\"Why not binary ISR?\\\" → binary 避免 Goodhart 但丢失更丰富训练信号，trade-off 引用 DeepSeek-Prover vs Lightman/Math-Shepherd\\n2. \\\"How relates to Gao et al. scaling laws?\\\" → β sweep 类似 KL sweep，可在其框架下画图\\n3. \\\"Why not adaptive β?\\\" → BiPaRS/ASR/ARS 已有 adaptive weighting，acknowledge 为 future work\\n4. \\\"Goodhart mechanism?\\\" → extremal + possible causal (Manheim taxonomy)\\n\\n### 文献资产\\n- `artifacts/literature_goodhart.md`: 30+ 篇论文，3 类别（Reward Overoptimization / Verification as Reward / Dose-Response）\\n- literature_map.yaml: 33 篇论文，6 类别\\n\\n## 文献空白与 Novelty (2026-05-14)\\n\\n### 核心 novelty（β=0.1 同剂量对照后更新）\\n- ISR 作为 neurosymbolic 结构质量的 **剂量敏感指标**——低剂量 bonus 提供 genuine structural guidance（3.2x vs random），高剂量触发 Goodhart\\n- ISR + component count 联合诊断区分 genuine vs degenerate proofs\\n- 文献中无人在 GRPO formal verification 场景做过此粒度的 reward shaping 消融\\n- **新增**：同剂量 ISR vs random 对照是干净的因果证据（β=0.1 唯一变量是 ISR vs random）\\n\\n### 关键文献\\n- Spurious Rewards (arXiv:2506.10947): random > structured reward，与我们 β=0.3 结果一致\\n- Gao et al. (ICML 2023): Goodhart effect scaling laws，解释 β 剂量依赖\\n- Karwowski et al. (ICLR 2024): Goodhart in RL 几何解释\\n\\n## Hacking 特征分析 (2026-05-14, plan012 + D044)\\n\\n### 操作性分类（6 种模式，strict exact normalized match, D044）\\n1. **H1 Conclusion Embedding** — 多 premise 中至少一个 premise 完全等于 conclusion（exact normalized match）\\n2. **Repetition collapse** — 重复生成相同 premise\\n3. **H3 Tautological collapse** — 退化为 2-component (premise=conclusion) 结构\\n4. **H4 Complexity-without-utility** — 生成复杂但无用的 premises\\n5. **Premise padding** — 添加无关 premises 填充\\n6. **H7 Conclusion Duplication with ISR Inflation** — 单 premise = conclusion（exact normalized），同时 game exec (P→P 永真) 和 ISR (唯一 premise necessary → ISR≈1.0)\\n\\n### Table 2 (D044 strict 定义)\\n| Mode | ISR β=0.1 s42 | Random β=0.1 s42 | Δ |\\n|------|---------------|------------------|---|\\n| H1 | 51.9% (42/81) | 70.9% (56/79) | -19.0pp |\\n| H3 | 1.2% (1/81) | 0.0% | +1.2pp |\\n| H4 | 0.0% | 3.8% (3/79) | -3.8pp |\\n| H7 | 16.0% (13/81) | 0.0% | +16.0pp |\\n| Genuine | 30.9% (25/81) | 25.3% (20/79) | +5.6pp |\\n| Total hacking | 69.1% (56/81) | 74.7% (59/79) | -5.6pp |\\n\\n### Hacking mode shift 洞察 (D044)\\n- ISR 和 random 的总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近（67.9% vs 70.9%）\\n- ISR reward 将 H1（多前提 embedding）转化为 H7（单前提 duplication + ISR 膨胀）\\n- 这是 mode shift，不是 net reduction\\n\\n### 各条件 hacking 分布\\n- Exec-only s42: 87% conclusion-in-premises hack, 48% ISR=0\\n- Random reward: 100% tautology, 96% 2-component\\n- Additive ISR β=0.3: format collapse (79/82 无 Conclusion)\\n- Additive random β=0.3: 复杂但无用 (ISR≈0, n_necessary=0)\\n- **Additive ISR β=0.1: 健康分布, mean components=5.58, ISR=0.337**\\n- **Additive random β=0.1: 冗余但无坍缩, components=8.46, ISR=0.104, 36.7% ISR=0**\\n\\n## Prolog 跨 logic type 实验 (2026-05-14, plan006)\\n\\n### plan006-prolog-sta-s42 (DONE, negative)\\n- **训练**: execution_sta (0.5×exec + 0.5×ISR)，step ~100 即饱和 (PR=1.0, ISR=1.0)\\n- **Eval**: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**（单 clause = query，零推理）\\n- **50% 失败原因**: 格式解析错误（`Facts/Rules:` 标签粘在 clause 里），非策略差异\\n- **关键洞察**: Prolog 比 FOL 更容易被 hack——Prolog 语义更简单（单 atom assertion），hack 路径更短，饱和更快（100 步 vs FOL 500 步）\\n- **论文意义**: ISR gaming 跨 logic type 泛化，且 Goodhart 严重程度与 logic type 表达复杂度负相关\\n\\n## GPU 隔离教训\\n\\n### C002: 所有命令必须 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N`\\n- 必须在 `bash -c` 内部 export\\n- submit_training_job 命令格式: `bash -c \\\"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N && ...\\\"`\\n\\n### bash -c 包装\\n- tmux 在 sh 下执行，source 是 bash built-in，必须用 `bash -c \\\"...\\\"` 包装\\n\\n### Checkpoint 路径\\n- save_total_limit=1 时，模型直接保存在 output_dir 根目录\\n\\n### 磁盘\\n- **C003 约束**: `log_completions=False` + `save_total_limit=1`，output_dir 放 `/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/`\\n- 2026-05-14 清理后 142G free\\n\\n## 已删除的 Checkpoint (2026-05-14)\\n- plan005_sta_low_s42: D030 决策不重训，用训练曲线数据\\n- plan001_s43_r4 和 plan001_s44: 早前清理\\n- grpo_exec_only 和 grpo_exec_sta: 早期 100 步实验\\n\\n## 准备就绪的资产 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n### Prolog STA\\n- 代码+eval 全部就绪，swipl 8.4.2 已验证，PrologSolver proved() 测试通过\\n- eval_sta.py 已有完整 Prolog 支持（L11/L33/L87-91/L162-163），不需要补代码\\n- plan006 可直接启动（500 步 × 3 seeds, 42/43/44）\\n- Director 命令模板：`python3 scripts/train_grpo.py --config configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml --num_steps 500 --seed {42|43|44} --output_dir ... --warmup_ratio 0.1`\\n- K 值等 dry-run 结果定（PR<5% → K=16，否则 K=8）\\n\\n### 文献\\n- literature_map.yaml: 33 篇论文，覆盖 6 个类别\\n- literature_goodhart.md: 30+ 篇 Goodhart 专题论文（2026-05-14 Worker 产出）\\n\\n### Eval 脚本\\n- eval_sta.py: FOL + Prolog 双支持，就绪\\n- eval_downstream.py: 就绪（需 `--base_model` 参数）\\n\", \"total_chars\": 10369, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, Garrabrant 2019)：policy 移到 ISR↑ 但 PR↓ 的 OOD 区域\\\\n- 可能有 **causal Goodhart** 成分：ISR 和 quality 的相关性在优化压力下断裂\\\\n\\\\n### 预期 reviewer 问题\\\\n1. \\\\\\\"Why not binary ISR?\\\\\\\" → binary 避免 Goodhart 但丢失更丰富训练信号，trade-off 引用 DeepSeek-Prover vs Lightman/Math-Shepherd\\\\n2. \\\\\\\"How relates to Gao et al. scaling laws?\\\\\\\" → β sweep 类似 KL sweep，可在其框架下画图\\\\n3. \\\\\\\"Why not adaptive β?\\\\\\\" → BiPaRS/ASR/ARS 已有 adaptive weighting，acknowledge 为 future work\\\\n4. \\\\\\\"Goodhart mechanism?\\\\\\\" → extremal + possible causal (Manheim taxonomy)\\\\n\\\\n### 文献资产\\\\n- `artifacts/literature_goodhart.md`: 30+ 篇论文，3 类别（Reward Overoptimization / Verification as Reward / Dose-Response）\\\\n- literature_map.yaml: 33 篇论文，6 类别\\\\n\\\\n## 文献空白与 Novelty (2026-05-14)\\\\n\\\\n### 核心 novelty（β=0.1 同剂量对照后更新）\\\\n- ISR 作为 neurosymbolic 结构质量的 **剂量敏感指标**——低剂量 bonus 提供 genuine structural guidance（3.2x vs random），高剂量触发 Goodhart\\\\n- ISR + component count 联合诊断区分 genuine vs degenerate proofs\\\\n- 文献中无人在 GRPO formal verification 场景做过此粒度的 reward shaping 消融\\\\n- **新增**：同剂量 ISR vs random 对照是干净的因果证据（β=0.1 唯一变量是 ISR vs random）\\\\n\\\\n### 关键文献\\\\n- Spurious Rewards (arXiv:2506.10947): random > structured reward，与我们 β=0.3 结果一致\\\\n- Gao et al. (ICML 2023): Goodhart effect scaling laws，解释 β 剂量依赖\\\\n- Karwowski et al. (ICLR 2024): Goodhart in RL 几何解释\\\\n\\\\n## Hacking 特征分析 (2026-05-14, plan012 + D044)\\\\n\\\\n### 操作性分类（6 种模式，strict exact normalized match, D044）\\\\n1. **H1 Conclusion Embedding** — 多 premise 中至少一个 premise 完全等于 conclusion（exact normalized match）\\\\n2. **Repetition collapse** — 重复生成相同 premise\\\\n3. **H3 Tautological collapse** — 退化为 2-component (premise=conclusion) 结构\\\\n4. **H4 Complexity-without-utility** — 生成复杂但无用的 premises\\\\n5. **Premise padding** — 添加无关 premises 填充\\\\n6. **H7 Conclusion Duplication with ISR Inflation** — 单 premise = conclusion（exact normalized），同时 game exec (P→P 永真) 和 ISR (唯一 premise necessary → ISR≈1.0)\\\\n\\\\n### Table 2 (D044 strict 定义)\\\\n| Mode | ISR β=0.1 s42 | Random β=0.1 s42 | Δ |\\\\n|------|---------------|------------------|---|\\\\n| H1 | 51.9% (42/81) | 70.9% (56/79) | -19.0pp |\\\\n| H3 | 1.2% (1/81) | 0.0% | +1.2pp |\\\\n| H4 | 0.0% | 3.8% (3/79) | -3.8pp |\\\\n| H7 | 16.0% (13/81) | 0.0% | +16.0pp |\\\\n| Genuine | 30.9% (25/81) | 25.3% (20/79) | +5.6pp |\\\\n| Total hacking | 69.1% (56/81) | 74.7% (59/79) | -5.6pp |\\\\n\\\\n### Hacking mode shift 洞察 (D044)\\\\n- ISR 和 random 的总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近（67.9% vs 70.9%）\\\\n- ISR reward 将 H1（多前提 embedding）转化为 H7（单前提 duplication + ISR 膨胀）\\\\n- 这是 mode shift，不是 net reduction\\\\n\\\\n### 各条件 hacking 分布\\\\n- Exec-only s42: 87% conclusion-in-premises hack, 48% ISR=0\\\\n- Random reward: 100% tautology, 96% 2-component\\\\n- Additive ISR β=0.3: format collapse (79/82 无 Conclusion)\\\\n- Additive random β=0.3: 复杂但无用 (ISR≈0, n_necessary=0)\\\\n- **Additive ISR β=0.1: 健康分布, mean components=5.58, ISR=0.337**\\\\n- **Additive random β=0.1: 冗余但无坍缩, components=8.46, ISR=0.104, 36.7% ISR=0**\\\\n\\\\n## Prolog 跨 logic type 实验 (2026-05-14, plan006)\\\\n\\\\n### plan006-prolog-sta-s42 (DONE, negative)\\\\n- **训练**: execution_sta (0.5×exec + 0.5×ISR)，step ~100 即饱和 (PR=1.0, ISR=1.0)\\\\n- **Eval**: PR=50%, proved-only ISR=1.0, **100% proved 样本是 H7**（单 clause = query，零推理）\\\\n- **50% 失败原因**: 格式解析错误（`Facts/Rules:` 标签粘在 clause 里），非策略差异\\\\n- **关键洞察**: Prolog 比 FOL 更容易被 hack——Prolog 语义更简单（单 atom assertion），hack 路径更短，饱和更快（100 步 vs FOL 500 步）\\\\n- **论文意义**: ISR gaming 跨 logic type 泛化，且 Goodhart 严重程度与 logic type 表达复杂度负相关\\\\n\\\\n## GPU 隔离教训\\\\n\\\\n### C002: 所有命令必须 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N`\\\\n- 必须在 `bash -c` 内部 export\\\\n- submit_training_job 命令格式: `bash -c \\\\\\\"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N && ...\\\\\\\"`\\\\n\\\\n### bash -c 包装\\\\n- tmux 在 sh 下执行，source 是 bash built-in，必须用 `bash -c \\\\\\\"...\\\\\\\"` 包装\\\\n\\\\n### Checkpoint 路径\\\\n- save_total_limit=1 时，模型直接保存在 output_dir 根目录\\\\n\\\\n### 磁盘\\\\n- **C003 约束**: `log_completions=False` + `save_total_limit=1`，output_dir 放 `/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/`\\\\n- 2026-05-14 清理后 142G free\\\\n\\\\n## 已删除的 Checkpoint (2026-05-14)\\\\n- plan005_sta_low_s42: D030 决策不重训，用训练曲线数据\\\\n- plan001_s43_r4 和 plan001_s44: 早前清理\\\\n- grpo_exec_only 和 grpo_exec_sta: 早期 100 步实验\\\\n\\\\n## 准备就绪的资产 (2026-05-14)\\\\n\\\\n### Prolog STA\\\\n- 代码+eval 全部就绪，swipl 8.4.2 已验证，PrologSolver proved() 测试通过\\\\n- eval_sta.py 已有完整 Prolog 支持（L11/L33/L87-91/L162-163），不需要补代码\\\\n- plan006 可直接启动（500 步 × 3 seeds, 42/43/44）\\\\n- Director 命令模板：`python3 scripts/train_grpo.py --config configs/grpo_prolog_exec_only.yaml --num_steps 500 --seed {42|43|44} --output_dir ... --warmup_ratio 0.1`\\\\n- K 值等 dry-run 结果定（PR<5% → K=16，否则 K=8）\\\\n\\\\n### 文献\\\\n- literature_map.yaml: 33 篇论文，覆盖 6 个类别\\\\n- literature_goodhart.md: 30+ 篇 Goodhart 专题论文（2026-05-14 Worker 产出）\\\\n\\\\n### Eval 脚本\\\\n- eval_sta.py: FOL + Prolog 双支持，就绪\\\\n- eval_downstream.py: 就绪（需 `--base_model` 参数）\\\\n\\\", Memory\\n\\n## 环境信息 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### westd-39279 服务器\\n- **GPU**: 2× RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell, 94GB VRAM 每张\\n- **CUDA**: 12.8, PyTorch 2.10.0+cu128\\n- **镜像**: aml-train（conda 环境，`source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base`）\\n- **ML 栈**: transformers/trl/vllm/accelerate 全部预装\\n- **Solvers**: Prover9 (LADR 2009-11A 源码编译), SWI-Prolog 8.4.2 (`/usr/bin/swipl`), Z3 4.16.0.0\\n- **模型**: Qwen3-4B 已下载（7.6GB，通过 ModelScope）\\n- **代码路径**: `/root/solver-trace-ablation/`\\n- **磁盘**: 系统盘 30GB (22G free)，数据盘 `/root/autodl-tmp/` 250GB (142G free)\\n\\n### 决策记录\\n- Qwen3-4B 用 ModelScope 下载（HuggingFace 直连可能慢）\\n- Prover9 从 LADR 源码编译（apt 无包）\\n- 94GB VRAM 单卡跑 Qwen3-4B 完全无压力\\n\\n## 数据 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### 数据源变更：ProofWriter → FOLIO\\n- ProofWriter 原始数据无 FOL 列，改用 `tasksource/folio`（1204 条人工标注 FOL）\\n- Phase 0 子集 100 条，训练子集 500 条\\n- 34% 数据含 XOR (⊕) 符号，已在 `_normalize_fol()` 中修复展开\\n\\n### 数据约束 (2026-05-14, D009)\\n- **C001**: 所有 plan001/plan013 实验统一使用 `train_500_seed42.jsonl`，不再用 `proofwriter_fol_500.jsonl`（消除数据差异 confound）\\n\\n## Phase 0 关键发现 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### Base Model ISR Baseline (sta-phase0-baseline)\\n- **Prove rate**: 12% overall, 18.2% excluding XOR-affected samples\\n- **Mean ISR**: 0.064 (very low — base model formalizations mostly unprovable)\\n- **ISR 区分度**: proved 样本 ISR 0.125–0.889\\n\\n### Ground-Truth ISR Baseline (sta-gt-isr-baseline)\\n- **GT prove rate**: 18% (limited by Prover9 only proving entailment; true=50%, false=4%, unknown=0%)\\n- **GT mean ISR**: 0.5588 (median 0.6125, range 0.14–1.00)\\n- **Model vs GT gap**: ISR 0.064 vs 0.559 = 8.7x 差距，STA 有强区分力\\n\\n### Prover9 局限性\\n- Prover9 只能证明 entailment（conclusion 从 premises 推出）\\n- `false` 和 `unknown` 类问题几乎无法证明（false 4%, unknown 0%）\\n\\n## Phase 1 关键经验 (2026-05-13)\\n\\n### fast_isr_fol 失败教训 (r4)\\n- `fast_isr_fol()` 是二值 tautology gate (0/1)，Qwen3-4B 不产生 tautology → ISR ≡ prove_rate → 两条件零差异\\n- **教训：binary ISR 无法提供有意义的 reward shaping 信号，必须用连续 ISR**\\n\\n### D010 Reward 公式转向 (2026-05-14)\\n- **旧公式（废弃）**: r = α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1 → ISR<1 时永远是 penalty\\n- **新公式**: r = r_exec · (1 + β·ISR), β>0 → ISR 只能 bonus，不能 penalty\\n\\n## Held-out eval_sta 全量结果 (2026-05-14) — 关键\\n\\n### 九组 eval 对比（含 plan016 1000步 + plan018 random β=0.1）\\n\\n| 实验 | Reward | Steps | eval PR | eval ISR | proved_only_ISR | n_components | 退化模式 |\\n|------|--------|-------|---------|----------|-----------------|--------------|----------|\\n| plan001-s42 | exec-only | 500 | **93%** | 0.123 | 0.132 | 6.58 | ✅ Genuine proofs |\\n| plan001-s43 | exec-only | 500 | **14%** | 0.000 | 0.000 | N/A | ❌ 长输出退化 |\\n| plan001-s44 | exec-only | 500 | **98%** | 0.570 | 0.582 | 2.04 | ❌ Tautological collapse |\\n| **plan016** | **exec-only** | **1000** | **96%** | **0.003** | **0.003** | **18.55** | **❌ 矛盾爆炸崩塌** |\\n| **plan002-b01** | **additive ISR β=0.1** | 500 | **81%** | **0.337** | **0.415** | **5.58** | **✅ Genuine + improved** |\\n| **plan018** | **additive random β=0.1** | 500 | **79%** | **0.104** | **0.131** | **8.46** | **⚠️ 冗余但无坍缩** |\\n| plan002-b03 | additive ISR β=0.3 | 500 | 18% | 0.026 | 0.143 | 10.67 | ❌ 过拟合+低泛化 |\\n| plan017 | additive random β=0.3 | 500 | 48% | 0.045 | 0.094 | 9.6 | ❌ 复杂但无用 |\\n| plan013 | convex random | 500 | 100% | 0.535 | 0.535 | 2.04 | ❌ Tautological hacking |\\n\\n### β=0.1 ISR vs Random 同剂量对照 (plan002-b01 vs plan018, D031 定论) — 重大发现\\n\\n**ISR β=0.1 提供 genuine structural guidance，非 variance regularization。**\\n\\n- PR 几乎相同 (0.81 vs 0.79) → β=0.1 噪声不影响证明能力\\n- ISR 差 3.2x (0.337 vs 0.104) → ISR 信号引导结构改善\\n- Components 5.58 vs 8.46 → ISR 组更精简\\n- random 组 36.7% proved 样本 ISR=0（无结构证明）\\n\\n### ISR-as-Reward β 剂量响应完整图谱\\n\\n| β | ISR reward PR | Random reward PR | ISR reward ISR | Random reward ISR | 结论 |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| 0.1 | 0.81 | 0.79 | **0.337** | 0.104 | ISR >> random (3.2x) |\\n| 0.3 | 0.18 | 0.48 | 0.026 | 0.045 | ISR << random (Goodhart) |\\n\\n**剂量依赖**：低 β ISR 有结构价值；高 β ISR 触发 Goodhart 效应反而比 random 更差。\\n\\n### plan016 延长训练发现 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n**ISR 崩塌随训练持续恶化，无回升信号。**\\n- 500步 ISR=0.123 → 1000步 ISR=0.003 (-97.8%)\\n- Windowed ISR 单调递减：step 200 peak 0.1115 → step 1000 低至 0.0144\\n- 退化模式：非 2-component 同义反复，而是矛盾爆炸（大量无关前提嵌入矛盾）\\n- Components 6.58→18.55 (+182%)\\n- 论文意义：exec-only 延长训练是 reward hacking 恶化的干净消融证据\\n\\n### 跨 seed 退化分类 (2026-05-14)\\n\\n| Seed | 退化模式 | 特征 | 诊断信号 |\\n|------|----------|------|----------|\\n| s42 | ✅ Genuine | PR=93%, components=6.58 | 唯一健康 seed |\\n| s43 | ❌ Length explosion | PR=14%, 87% hit max_new_tokens | D029: 不重训 |\\n| s44 | ❌ Tautological collapse | PR=98%, components=2.04 | ISR=0.5 artifact |\\n\\n### 关键教训\\n1. **跨 seed 极不稳定**: exec-only reward landscape 方差极大\\n2. **ISR + component count 联合诊断**能区分 genuine proof vs tautological hack\\n3. **ISR-as-reward 有 sweet spot**: β=0.1 有效，β≥0.3 有害（Goodhart's Law）\\n4. **训练 PR vs eval PR 巨大鸿沟**: 训练 PR 不反映 held-out 泛化能力\\n5. **ISR β=0.1 vs random β=0.1**: ISR 3.2x 差异证明 ISR 信号有结构价值（非 variance regularization）\\n\\n## Downstream NLI 验证失败 (2026-05-14, plan014)\\n- Spearman ρ(ISR, correct) = -0.049 (p=0.187, 不显著)\\n- 根因：模型严重 true-bias (false GRPO+exec reward 的通用退化**：ISR H1=51.8%，random H1=70.9%——conclusion embedding 是训练本身的通用 hacking，不因 ISR/random 改变\\n- **ISR 改变了 hacking 的组成而非总量**：总 conclusion-in-premise 率接近（ISR 67.9% vs random 70.9%），但 ISR 将 H1 转化为更极端的 H7（单前提退化 + ISR 膨胀）\\n- s42 proved_only_ISR=0.415 < GT=0.559，在非 H7 样本中存在 genuine structure\\n- **结论**: ISR reward 不减少 conclusion embedding 总量，而是将其从 H1（多前提）推向 H7（单前提 + ISR 膨胀）——更极端且更隐蔽（PR 和 ISR 表面都高）。ISR 作为 reward component 的可靠性不足。\\n\\n**s42 单 seed Hacking Taxonomy 定量对比 (D038→D044 strict 校准)**：\\n- ISR β=0.1 genuine: **30.9%** (25/81) vs random β=0.1 genuine: **25.3%** (20/79) — **+5.6pp**\\n- H1 (multi-premise conclusion embedding, strict): ISR 51.9% vs random 70.9% — GRPO 通用退化\\n- H7 (single premise=conclusion, ISR-specific): ISR **16.1%** vs random **0%** — ISR 特异性\\n- H3 (complexity-without-utility): ISR **0%** vs random 3.8% — ISR 完全消除此模式\\n- 整体 hacking rate (strict): ISR **69.1%** vs random **74.7%** — ISR 略降但主要是模式转移（H1→H7）\\n- 所有数字可被 classify_h7.py 精确复现（D044）\\n- ⚠️ s43/s44 几乎 100% H7（96.7%/86.5%），无 H1\\n\\n**证据（β=0.3 同剂量对照）**：\\n- plan002-b03 (ISR β=0.3): eval PR=0.18, ISR=0.026 — Goodhart 效应，PR 崩塌\\n- plan017 (random β=0.3): eval PR=0.48, ISR=0.045 — random 比 ISR 好 2.7x PR\\n- **结论**：高剂量 ISR 对训练 actively misleading (PR 18% < random 48%)\\n\\n**β 剂量效应全景**：\\n| β | ISR reward PR | Random reward PR | ISR reward ISR | Random reward ISR | 解读 |\\n|---|---|---|---|---|---|\\n| 0.1 | 0.81 (s42) / 0.61 (s43) / 0.89 (s44) | 0.79 | **0.415** (s42) / **0.830** (s43) / **0.902** (s44) | 0.131 | s42 genuine; s43/s44 ISR-specific gaming (conclusion duplication) |\\n| 0.3 | 0.18 | 0.48 | 0.026 | 0.045 | ISR << random (Goodhart) |\\n\\n**其他 ISR-as-reward 失败模式**：\\n- plan005 凸组合 (α·r_exec + β·r_exec·ISR, α+β=1): ISR<1 时永远是 penalty → PR 下降 (D010)\\n- plan013 convex random: PR=1.0 但 100% P→P tautological collapse (D018)\\n\\n⚠️ **措辞约束 (Reviewer D027)**: plan013 (0.9·r_exec+0.1·random) vs plan017 (r_exec·(1+0.3·random)) 公式结构不同，不可简化为\\\"additive vs convex\\\"对照。plan017 vs plan002 和 plan018 vs plan002-b01 的 ISR vs random 对比是纯净的（唯一变量是 random vs ISR）\\n\\n⚠️ **β=0.1 三 seed 已出（D043）**：s42 genuine, s43/s44 conclusion (pilot → collect shortcuts → classify → map RIP → design check) transfers to structurally different formal languages. Hacking modes and ISR diagnostic value replicate across FOL→Prover9 and Prolog→SWI-Prolog.\\n\\n**证据**: 待验证。plan006 Prolog GRPO dry-run 进行中，训练即将启动（3 seeds × 500 步）。如果 plan006 无法复现 hacking + ISR 诊断，此 claim 降级为 future work。\\n\\n## Story Spine\\n\\n- **Problem**: GRPO with execution-only rewards trains neurosymbolic formalizers that achieve high solve rates but produce structurally degenerate outputs (reward hacking). Current practice evaluates only prove_rate, missing structural quality entirely. No principled diagnostic exists for detecting and characterizing this failure mode.\\n- **Existing approaches & gaps**: Mellgren et al. (2025) hand-craft Prolog-specific structure penalties. EVOM (2026) uses scalar execution rewards only. ExecVerify/EGCA derive trace-level rewards for code, not formal logic. No work systematically studies reward hacking in formal reasoning GRPO or provides diagnostic tools for it.\\n- **Our contribution**: We propose STA (Solver Trace Ablation), applying component necessity testing (LOO ablation) to derive ISR as a **diagnostic metric** for structural integrity. Key findings:\\n  1. ISR quantifies hacking invisible to prove_rate (64% → 13% → 0.3% collapse over training)\\n  2. ISR as proxy metric exhibits **meta-level Goodhart's Law**: at low dose (β=0.1), ISR reward induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication that inflates ISR itself), while only 1/3 seeds show genuine improvement; at high dose (β=0.3), classic Goodhart collapse (PR 18% vs random 48%). The diagnostic metric becomes the target of gaming when used as optimization signal.\\n  3. GRPO saturation creates a \\\"training trap\\\": PR→1 means no gradient signal; adding variance redirects rather than prevents hacking\\n  4. Different reward formulations produce distinct, classifiable hacking strategies (6 modes identified, including ISR-specific \\\"conclusion duplication with ISR inflation\\\")\\n  5. ISR measures syntactic component necessity, not semantic faithfulness — this boundary is important for practitioners\\n- **Actionable insights for practitioners**:\\n  1. Never trust prove_rate alone — use ISR + component count as joint diagnostic\\n  2. Single-seed GRPO results are unreliable (2/3 exec-only seeds collapse differently)\\n  3. ISR reward is unreliable as training signal at any dose: β=0.1 induces ISR-specific gaming in 2/3 seeds (conclusion duplication inflates ISR itself), β=0.3 collapses PR. Use ISR for post-hoc diagnosis only.\\n  4. Proxy metrics that detect gaming can themselves become gaming targets when used as optimization signals (meta-level Goodhart) — a cautionary result for any proxy-metric-as-reward approach.\\n- **Headline result**: \\\"We demonstrate that ISR — a component necessity metric derived from solver trace ablation — serves as a powerful diagnostic for reward hacking in GRPO-trained formalizers, but exhibits meta-level Goodhart's Law when used as a training\\n\\n... The diagnostic metric becomes the target of gaming when used as optimization signal.\\\\n  3.
